     4	const WINDOW_MS = 60_000;
     5	const MAX_SAMPLES = 32;
     6	const SHORT_RTT_MS = 1000;
     7	const clocks = new WeakMap<typeof fetch, ServerClock>();
     8	const readers = new WeakMap<() => number, ServerClock>();
     9	type Dispatch = { url: string; authorization: string | null };
    10	const responses = new WeakMap<Response, { clock: ServerClock; order: number; request: Dispatch }>();
    11	interface Sample { offset: number; roundTrip: number; received: number; provisional: boolean }
    12	
    13	/** One bounded, minimum-round-trip window, reset on a server clock revision.
    14	 * A clock advance must not compete with faster samples from before the jump. */
    15	export class ServerClock {
    16	  private offset = 0;
    17	  private issuedOffset: number | undefined;
    18	  private last = 0;
    19	  private samples: Sample[] = [];
    20	  private revision: string | undefined;
    21	  private order = 0;
    22	  private epochBarrier = 0;
    23	  private listeners = new Set<(shift: number, reschedule: boolean) => void>();
    24	  private responseListeners = new Set<(response: Response, request: Dispatch) => void | boolean | Promise<void | boolean>>();
    25	  private reconciling: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve();
    26	  private save: ((value: string) => Promise<void>) | undefined;
    27	  private saving: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve();
    28	  private lastSave: { owner: (value: string) => Promise<void>; value: string; result: Promise<void> } | undefined;
    29	  constructor(private wall: () => number = Date.now) { readers.set(this.issuedDeviceNow, this); }
    30	  fork(): ServerClock {
    31	    const copy = new ServerClock(this.wall);
    32	    copy.offset = this.offset; copy.issuedOffset = this.issuedOffset; copy.last = this.last;
    33	    copy.samples = this.samples.slice(); copy.revision = this.revision;
    34	    copy.order = this.order; copy.epochBarrier = this.epochBarrier;
    35	    return copy;
    36	  }
    37	  /** Unfloored estimated server time: samples and server-answer refetches. */
    38	  estimatedServerNow = (): number => Math.min(Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER, Math.max(0, Math.round(this.wall() + this.offset)));
    39	  private deviceCandidate = (): number => Math.min(Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER, Math.max(0, Math.round(this.wall() + (this.issuedOffset ?? 0))));
    40	  /** Monotonic issued device time: execution, prediction, expiry and rendering. */
    41	  issuedDeviceNow = (): number => this.last = Math.max(this.last, this.deviceCandidate());
    42	  /** Adopt a committed admission without changing the server-time estimate. */
    43	  raiseIssued(time: number): void {
    44	    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(time) || time < 0 || time <= this.last) return;
    45	    const shift = time - Math.max(this.last, this.deviceCandidate());
    46	    this.last = time;
    47	    // Even a one-millisecond floor increase may cross a frozen deadline.
    48	    if (shift > 0) for (const listener of this.listeners) listener(shift, true);
    49	  }
    50	  subscribe = (listener: (shift: number, reschedule: boolean) => void): (() => void) => {
    51	    this.listeners.add(listener);
    52	    return () => { this.listeners.delete(listener); };
    53	  };
    54	  sample(serverNow: number, sent: number, received: number, revision?: string, order = ++this.order, provisional = false) {
    55	    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(serverNow) || serverNow < 0 || !Number.isFinite(sent) || !Number.isFinite(received) || received < sent) return;
    56	    this.order = Math.max(this.order, order);
    57	    const revisionChanged = revision !== undefined && revision !== this.revision;
    58	    if (revisionChanged) {
    59	      // A delayed reply from before an advance cannot restore the old window.
    60	      const split = (value: string) => value.split(":");
    61	      const [epoch, version] = split(revision), [priorEpoch, priorVersion] = split(this.revision ?? "");
    62	      if (epoch === priorEpoch ? Number(version) < Number(priorVersion) : this.revision !== undefined && order <= this.epochBarrier) return;
    63	      this.samples = [];
    64	      this.revision = revision;
    65	      // Every request already issued may still have reached the old process.
    66	      if (epoch !== priorEpoch) this.epochBarrier = this.order;
    67	    }
    68	    const before = this.offset, issuedBefore = this.issuedOffset;
    69	    const roundTrip = received - sent;
    70	    // Long responses use a conservative receipt date, never a midpoint that
    71	    // could latch half a server wait into the monotonic floor.
    72	    provisional ||= roundTrip > SHORT_RTT_MS;
    73	    this.samples = this.samples.filter(s => received >= s.received && received - s.received <= WINDOW_MS).slice(-(MAX_SAMPLES - 1));
    74	    this.samples.push({ offset: serverNow - (provisional ? received : (sent + received) / 2), roundTrip, received, provisional });
    75	    // Every short measured sample outranks provisional receipt stamps.
    76	    const best = this.samples.reduce((best, s) => s.provisional !== best.provisional ? (best.provisional ? s : best) : s.roundTrip <= best.roundTrip ? s : best);
    77	    this.offset = best.offset;
    78	    this.issuedOffset = best.offset;
    79	    if (before !== this.offset || issuedBefore !== this.issuedOffset) {
    80	      void this.persist().catch(() => {}); // Device calls/close await the same durable save.
    81	      // Watchers ignore sub-frame noise. A newly eligible issued offset can
    82	      // move even when a short sample merely confirms the server estimate.
    83	      const estimateShift = this.offset - before, issuedShift = (this.issuedOffset ?? 0) - (issuedBefore ?? 0);
    84	      const shift = Math.abs(issuedShift) > Math.abs(estimateShift) ? issuedShift : estimateShift;
    85	      for (const listener of this.listeners) listener(shift, revisionChanged);
    86	    }
    87	  }
    88	  /** Credential metadata must be reconciled before a business-time sample
    89	   * can reschedule expiry. The immutable dispatch bearer fences rotations. */
    90	  beforeSample(listener: (response: Response, request: Dispatch) => void | boolean | Promise<void | boolean>): () => void {
    91	    this.responseListeners.add(listener);
    92	    return () => { this.responseListeners.delete(listener); };
    93	  }
    94	  /** Serialize credential custody and the corresponding clock publication.
    95	   * A failed save leaves both the durable and live clock at its prior value. */
    96	  reconcile(response: Response, request: Dispatch, publish: () => void): Promise<void> {
    97	    const work = this.reconciling.catch(() => {}).then(async () => {
    98	      if (response.headers.get("x-snapback-session-timing") === "unavailable") return;
    99	      for (const listener of this.responseListeners) if (await listener(response, request) === false) return;
   100	      publish();
   101	    });
   102	    this.reconciling = work;
   103	    return work;
   104	  }
   105	  wrap(fetcher: typeof fetch): typeof fetch {
   106	    if (clocks.get(fetcher) === this) return fetcher;
   107	    const wrapped: typeof fetch = async (input, init) => {
   108	      const sent = this.wall(), order = ++this.order;
   109	      const request = { url: typeof input === "string" ? input : input instanceof URL ? input.href : input.url,
   110	        authorization: new Headers(init?.headers ?? (input instanceof Request ? input.headers : undefined)).get("authorization") };
   111	      const response = await fetcher(input, init);
   112	      const received = this.wall();
   113	      // An unavailable staging authority snapshot cannot publish business time
   114	      // without its credential lifetime. Stream-final samples obey the same rule.
   115	      if (response.headers.get("x-snapback-session-timing") === "unavailable") return response;
   116	      await this.reconcile(response, request, () => {
   117	        responses.set(response, { clock: this, order, request });
   118	        const stamp = response.headers.get("x-snapback-time");
   119	        if (stamp !== null) this.sample(Number(stamp), sent, received, response.headers.get("x-snapback-clock") ?? undefined, order);
   120	      });
   121	      return response;
   122	    };
   123	    clocks.set(wrapped, this);
   124	    return wrapped;
   125	  }
   126	  serverDelay(deadline: number): number { return Math.max(0, deadline - this.estimatedServerNow()); }
   127	  deviceDelay(deadline: number): number {
   128	    return this.issuedDeviceNow() >= deadline ? 0 : Math.max(0, deadline - this.deviceCandidate());
   129	  }
   130	  sampleStream(serverNow: number, revision: string | undefined, order: number) {
   131	    const received = this.wall();
   132	    // The server writes this stamp at the end of its hold, not at request send.
   133	    this.sample(serverNow, received, received, revision, order, true);
   134	  }
   135	  async attach(core: DeviceCore) {
   136	    const kept = await core.call({ op: "meta", key: "server-clock" });
   137	    if (kept.denied) throw new Error(JSON.stringify(kept.denied));
   138	    try {
   139	      const state = JSON.parse(String(kept.ok)) as { offset: number; issuedOffset?: number } | null;
   140	      const issuedOffset = state?.issuedOffset ?? state?.offset;
   141	      if (state && Number.isFinite(state.offset) && Math.abs(state.offset) <= Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER && typeof issuedOffset === "number" && Number.isFinite(issuedOffset) && Math.abs(issuedOffset) <= Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER) {
   142	        if (!this.samples.length) this.offset = state.offset;
   143	        this.issuedOffset ??= issuedOffset;
   144	      }
   145	    } catch { /* Missing or malformed clock metadata starts at offset zero. */ }
   146	    // Only admissions restore a durable floor. Old offset metadata's `last`
   147	    // may be a wall-time now() call and must not override a startup correction.
   148	    // The device commits this floor atomically with admission, and retains it
   149	    // after settlement. Pending entries also recover older stores/journals.
   150	    // Backfill legacy admission times transactionally before replay can retire
   151	    // the queue; offset persistence deliberately cannot retain an issued floor.
   152	    const pending = await core.call({ op: "queued", restore_clock_floor: true });
   153	    if (pending.denied) throw new Error(JSON.stringify(pending.denied));
   154	    const floor = await core.call({ op: "meta", key: "server-clock-floor" });
   155	    if (floor.denied) throw new Error(JSON.stringify(floor.denied));
   156	    const issued = Number(floor.ok);
   157	    if (Number.isSafeInteger(issued) && issued >= 0) this.last = Math.max(this.last, issued);
   158	    for (const entry of (pending.ok ?? []) as { now?: number }[]) {
   159	      if (Number.isSafeInteger(entry.now) && entry.now! >= 0) this.last = Math.max(this.last, entry.now!);
   160	    }
   161	    this.save = async value => {
   162	      const reply = await core.call({ op: "set_meta", key: "server-clock", value });
   163	      if (reply.denied) throw new Error(JSON.stringify(reply.denied));
   164	    };
   165	    await this.persist();
   166	  }
   167	  // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.3 — share unchanged offset persistence, never admission floors.
   168	  persist(): Promise<void> {
   169	    if (!this.save) return Promise.resolve();
   170	    const save = this.save, value = JSON.stringify({ offset: this.offset, issuedOffset: this.issuedOffset ?? 0 });
   171	    if (this.lastSave?.owner === save && this.lastSave.value === value) return this.lastSave.result;
   172	    this.saving = this.saving.catch(() => {}).then(() => save(value));
   173	    const saved = this.lastSave = { owner: save, value, result: this.saving };
   174	    // A failed save remains a failure to its callers, and is retried by the
   175	    // next caller. Attaching another device always supplies a new owner.
   176	    void this.saving.catch(() => { if (this.lastSave === saved) this.lastSave = undefined; });
   177	    return this.saving;
   178	  }
   179	  async detach() { const pending = this.persist(); this.save = undefined; await pending; }
   180	}
   181	
   182	export const serverClock = (fetcher: typeof fetch): ServerClock => clocks.get(fetcher) ?? new ServerClock();
   183	
   184	/** Streaming final stamps are provisional receipt-time samples, never poll midpoints. */
   185	export async function sampleChanges(response: Response, body: { serverNow?: number; clockRevision?: string; sessionTiming?: string; sessionExpiresAt?: number; sessionCapExpiresAt?: number }) {
   186	  const sample = responses.get(response);
   187	  if (!sample || body.sessionTiming === "unavailable") return;
   188	  const headers = new Headers();
   189	  if (body.sessionTiming !== undefined) headers.set("x-snapback-session-timing", body.sessionTiming);
   190	  if (body.sessionExpiresAt !== undefined) headers.set("x-snapback-session-expires", String(body.sessionExpiresAt));
   191	  if (body.sessionCapExpiresAt !== undefined) headers.set("x-snapback-session-cap", String(body.sessionCapExpiresAt));
   192	  await sample.clock.reconcile(new Response(null, { headers }), sample.request, () => {
   193	    if (body.serverNow !== undefined) sample.clock.sampleStream(body.serverNow, body.clockRevision, sample.order);
   194	  });
   195	}
   196	
   197	export function onClock(now: () => number, listener: () => void): () => void {
   198	  return readers.get(now)?.subscribe(listener) ?? (() => {});
   199	}
   200	
   201	export function clockDelay(now: () => number, deadline: number): number {
   202	  return readers.get(now)?.deviceDelay(deadline) ?? Math.max(0, deadline - now());
   203	}

     1	import type { Session } from "./auth.ts";
     2	import { isReadToken } from "./read-token.ts";
     3	
     4	/** Only classified staging returns this exact bearer's current live lifetime.
     5	 * Matching the dispatch bearer is the caller's responsibility. Same-bearer
     6	 * preservation is monotonic, so a delayed old reply cannot undo a jump. */
     7	export function reconciledTiming(session: Session, response: Response): Session {
     8	  if (response.headers.get("x-snapback-session-timing") !== "staging") return session;
     9	  const expiry = Number(response.headers.get("x-snapback-session-expires"));
    10	  const capText = response.headers.get("x-snapback-session-cap");
    11	  const cap = capText === null ? undefined : Number(capText);
    12	  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(expiry) || expiry <= 0) return session;
    13	  const read = isReadToken(session.token);
    14	  if (read && (cap === undefined || !Number.isSafeInteger(cap) || cap < expiry)) return session;
    15	  if (!read && cap !== undefined) return session;
    16	  const expiresAt = Math.max(session.expiresAt, expiry);
    17	  const capExpiresAt = cap === undefined ? undefined : Math.max(session.capExpiresAt ?? 0, cap);
    18	  if (expiresAt === session.expiresAt && capExpiresAt === session.capExpiresAt) return session;
    19	  return { ...session, expiresAt, ...(capExpiresAt === undefined ? {} : { capExpiresAt }) };
    20	}
    21	
    22	export function requestMatchesSession(request: { url: string; authorization: string | null }, base: string, session: Session): boolean {
    23	  return (request.url === base || request.url.startsWith(base + "/")) && request.authorization === `Bearer ${session.token}`;
    24	}
    25	
    26	/** Expired local metadata may be superseded by a preserving staging jump.
    27	 * Read-only probing creates no session and never widens or refreshes one. */
    28	export async function restoreStagingTiming(base: string, session: Session, fetcher: typeof fetch): Promise<Session | null> {
    29	  const aborter = new AbortController();
    30	  const timer = setTimeout(() => aborter.abort(), 10_000);
    31	  (timer as { unref?: () => void }).unref?.();
    32	  try {
    33	    const response = await fetcher(`${base}/probe?scope=1`, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${session.token}` }, signal: aborter.signal });
    34	    if (!response.ok) return null;
    35	    const next = reconciledTiming(session, response);
    36	    const stamp = Number(response.headers.get("x-snapback-time"));
    37	    if (next === session || !Number.isSafeInteger(stamp) || next.expiresAt <= stamp) return null;
    38	    // Consume the bounded ordinary probe body before blessing restoration.
    39	    const { readResponse } = await import("./http-response.ts");
    40	    const body = await readResponse(response);
    41	    return body.ok === true && body.viewer === session.principal && body.scope === (isReadToken(session.token) ? "read" : "full") ? next : null;
    42	  } catch { return null; }
    43	  finally { clearTimeout(timer); }
    44	}

   294	pub(super) fn maintain(shared: Arc<Shared>) {
   295	    let mut tick = 0;
   296	    let mut last_tick = Instant::now();
   297	    loop {
   298	        if crate::lifecycle::stopping() { return; }
   299	        {
   300	            let state = lock(&shared.maintenance);
   301	            if !state.advancing && !state.paused && let Err(error) = shared.ephemeral.sweep(shared.now()) { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: ephemeral sweep: {error}"); }
   302	        }
   303	        if last_tick.elapsed() >= Duration::from_secs(1) {
   304	            tick += 1; last_tick = Instant::now();
   305	            // One bounded reclamation slice; abandoned bytes remain charged
   306	            // until their actual records are dropped. No engine lock is held.
   307	            lock(&shared.following).reclaim(shared.now(), snapback4_core::Ceilings { heap_bytes: 1 << 20, ..Default::default() });
   308	            if let Err(error) = timely(&shared, tick) { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: maintenance failed: {error}"); }
   309	        }
   310	        promote_aged(&shared, Instant::now());
   311	        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(100));
   312	    }
   313	}
   314	
   315	fn promote_aged(shared: &Shared, now: Instant) {
   316	    if lock(&shared.maintenance).eligible(now, true) { bulk_slice(shared, true); }
   317	}
   318	
   319	pub(super) fn bulk(shared: Arc<Shared>) {
   320	    loop {
   321	        if crate::lifecycle::stopping() { return; }
   322	        let observed = *lock(&shared.seq);
   323	        let mut did_work = false;
   324	        for _ in 0..3 {
   325	            if crate::lifecycle::stopping() { return; }
   326	            did_work |= bulk_slice(&shared, false);
   327	            let pause = lock(&shared.maintenance).resume_at.saturating_duration_since(Instant::now());
   328	            if !pause.is_zero() {
   329	                // No shared lock survives this pause. Request commits do not
   330	                // cancel repayment; empty kinds do not idle an active cycle.
   331	                std::thread::sleep(pause.min(Duration::from_millis(100)));
   332	            }
   333	        }
   334	        if !did_work {
   335	            loop {
   336	                let pause = lock(&shared.maintenance).empty_cycle(Instant::now());
   337	                if pause.is_zero() { break; }
   338	                std::thread::sleep(pause.min(Duration::from_millis(100)));
   339	            }
   340	            let seq = lock(&shared.seq);
   341	            if *seq == observed {
   342	                drop(shared.committed.wait_timeout(seq, Duration::from_millis(100)).unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner()));
   343	            }
   344	        }
   345	    }
   346	}

   348	// Reset even when a debug/unwind request panics mid-drain. Engine poison
   349	// recovery and durable work cursors retain the normal retry semantics.
   350	struct Advancing<'a>(&'a Shared);
   351	impl Drop for Advancing<'_> {
   352	    fn drop(&mut self) {
   353	        let mut state = lock(&self.0.maintenance);
   354	        state.advancing = false;
   355	        state.next = BulkKind::Delivery;
   356	    }
   357	}

   359	fn completed_drain(work: Work, stopping: bool) -> Result<Work, snapback4_core::Refusal> {
   360	    if stopping {
   361	        return Err(snapback4_core::Refusal::new("E_CLOCK_INTERRUPTED", "clock", "clock advanced, but host shutdown interrupted the drain; inspect queued work instead of repeating the advance").guide("clock"));
   362	    }
   363	    Ok(work)
   364	}

   366	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#4-staging-clock — staging grants clock control, never fixture or persona authority.
   367	pub(super) fn staging(shared: &Shared) -> bool {
   368	    lock(&shared.engine).store().deployment_environment().ok().flatten().as_deref() == Some("staging")
   369	}
   370	fn clock_service(request: &Request, shared: &Shared) -> Result<Option<String>, snapback4_core::Refusal> {
   371	    if shared.personas { return Ok(None); }
   372	    if !staging(shared) { return Err(crate::dev_clock::release()); }
   373	    match super::session_identity::principal_of(request,shared) {
   374	        Ok((principal,kind,snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Full)) if kind == "service" && principal.starts_with("service:") => Ok(Some(principal)),
   375	        _ => Err(snapback4_core::Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "staging clock control requires a full service token").guide("clock")),
   376	    }
   377	}
   378	pub(super) fn advance(request: Request, shared: &Shared, body: &str) {
   379	    let service = match clock_service(&request, shared) {
   380	        Ok(service) => service,
   381	        Err(why) => return respond(request,403,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)})),
   382	    };
   383	    let payload = match serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(body) {
   384	        Ok(payload) => payload,
   385	        Err(_) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("clock advance takes {\"duration\":\"+2m\"}"))})),
   386	    };
   387	    let each_boundary = match payload.get("eachBoundary") {
   388	        None => false,
   389	        Some(serde_json::Value::Bool(value)) => *value,
   390	        Some(_) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("eachBoundary must be a boolean"))})),
   391	    };
   392	    let boolean = |key: &str, default: bool| -> Result<bool, snapback4_core::Refusal> {
   393	        match payload.get(key) {
   394	            None => Ok(default), Some(serde_json::Value::Bool(value)) => Ok(*value),
   395	            Some(_) => Err(crate::dev_clock::input(format!("{key} must be a boolean"))),
   396	        }
   397	    };
   398	    let (drain, allow_held) = match boolean("drain", true).and_then(|drain| Ok((drain, boolean("allowHeld", false)?))) {
   399	        Ok(options) => options,
   400	        Err(error) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
   401	    };
   402	    if each_boundary && !drain { return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("eachBoundary requires drain:true"))})); }
   403	    let preserve_sessions = match payload.get("sessions").and_then(|s| s.as_str()) {
   404	        None if payload.get("sessions").is_none() => !shared.personas,
   405	        Some("preserve") if !shared.personas => true,
   406	        Some("expire") => false,
   407	        _ => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("sessions must be preserve (staging only) or expire"))})),
   408	    };
   409	    let duration = payload.get("duration").and_then(|value| value.as_str());
   410	    let milliseconds = match duration.ok_or_else(|| crate::dev_clock::input("clock advance takes {\"duration\":\"+2m\"}"))
   411	        .and_then(|duration| if duration == "0ms" && drain { Ok(0) } else { crate::dev_clock::duration(duration) }) {
   412	        Ok(milliseconds) => milliseconds,
   413	        Err(error) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
   414	    };
   415	    // Serialize clock callers only. Background slices check advancing after
   416	    // engine admission and skip during this synchronous drain.
   417	    let advance = lock(&shared.maintenance).advance.clone();
   418	    let _advance = lock(&advance);
   419	    let _advancing = Advancing(shared);
   420	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12 — simulated time makes retries due without stealing a live worker's claim or viewer session.
   421	    let advanced = (|| {
   422	        // Match claims' engine-then-clock lock order: no claim can land
   423	        // between shifting its lease and publishing the advanced clock.
   424	        let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
   425	        let before = crate::dev_clock_store::saved_boundaries(&engine);
   426	        if let Some(principal) = &service {
   427	            if engine.store().deployment_environment().map_err(|e|e.refusal())?.as_deref() != Some("staging") { return Err(crate::dev_clock::release()); }
   428	            super::session_identity::mutation_authority(&request,shared,&engine,principal)?;
   429	        } else { shared.dev_authority(&engine)?; }
   430	        let mut clock = lock(&shared.clock);
   431	        let floor = shared.time_audience_clock.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Acquire);
   432	        let effective_before = clock.now().max(floor);
   433	        let mut next = clock.clone();
   434	        let now = (if milliseconds == 0 { next.now() } else { next.advance(milliseconds)? }).max(floor);
   435	        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#11-wave-22-loop-repairs-dx6 — refuse a saved boundary before any clock/session effect.
   436	        if drain && !allow_held && !shared.hold_schedules {
   437	            crate::dev_clock_store::refuse_held(&engine, &before, now)?;
   438	        }
   439	        let boundaries = crate::dev_clock_store::crossed_boundaries(&engine, &before, now, shared.hold_schedules || !drain, each_boundary)?;
   440	        lock(&shared.maintenance).advancing = true;
   441	        if !shared.personas {
   442	            // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#11-wave-22-loop-repairs-dx6 — a retained disclosure floor is also the credential authority clock.
   443	            // A raw jump below that floor advances no business time and cannot
   444	            // extend credentials or leases. Selection must never see an older
   445	            // raw process time and revive an already expired family.
   446	            let effective_delta = now.checked_sub(effective_before)
   447	                .ok_or_else(|| crate::dev_clock::input("effective clock advance overflow"))?;
   448	            if milliseconds > 0 && effective_delta > 0 { engine.advance_staging_clock_at(effective_delta, effective_before, preserve_sessions).map_err(|e|e.refusal())?; }
   449	        } else if milliseconds > 0 {
   450	            engine.advance_worker_leases_at(milliseconds, clock.now()).map_err(|e|e.refusal())?;
   451	        }
   452	        lock(&shared.maintenance).paused = true;
   453	        *clock = next;
   454	        let offset = clock.offset();
   455	        let skew = now - snapback4_core::now_ms();
   456	        let isolated = if crate::dev_clock_store::has_data(&engine) {
   457	            format!("; use {} for isolated time tests; clock advance --port N targets its printed port (guide jobs)", crate::dev_clock::isolated_command())
   458	        } else { String::new() };
   459	        let message = format!("cumulative offset {offset:+}ms (requested process offset; now includes the authoritative floor); process offset lasts until restart{isolated}");
   460	        Ok((now, offset, skew, message, before, boundaries))
   461	    })();
   462	    let (now, offset, skew, message, before, boundaries) = match advanced {
   463	        Ok(result) => result,
   464	        Err(error) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
   465	    };
   466	    // @ref LLP 3000#contended-read-serving — a developer is awaiting this
   467	    // request; synchronous clock-advance work intentionally keeps request QoS.
   468	    let drained = (|| {
   469	        if !drain { return Ok(Work::default()); }
   470	        let mut crossed = Vec::new();
   471	        if each_boundary {
   472	            for (index, at) in boundaries {
   473	                crossed.extend(engine_work(shared, |engine, _| engine.fire_schedule_boundary_at(shared.now(), index, at))?);
   474	            }
   475	        }
   476	        let mut work = work_at(shared, true, 0, Some(now))?;
   477	        work.schedules += crossed.len();
   478	        work.fired.extend(crossed);
   479	        Ok::<_, RuntimeError>(work)
   480	    })();
   481	    match drained.map_err(|error| error.refusal()).and_then(|work| completed_drain(work, crate::lifecycle::stopping())) {
   482	        Ok(work) => {
   483	            lock(&shared.maintenance).paused = !drain;
   484	            let schedules = crate::dev_clock_store::schedule_report(&lock(&shared.engine), &before, &work.fired, now, shared.hold_schedules || !drain, each_boundary, shared.dev_identity.as_ref().map(|i| i.address.port()));
   485	            let (fired, held) = crate::dev_clock_store::outcomes(&schedules);
   486	            respond(request, 200, json!({"now":now, "advancedMs":milliseconds, "sessionNotice":if preserve_sessions { crate::dev_clock::PRESERVED_SESSION_NOTICE } else { crate::dev_clock::SESSION_NOTICE }, "maintenancePaused":!drain, "sessionsPreserved":preserve_sessions, "offsetMs":offset, "skewMs":skew, "eachBoundary":each_boundary, "message":message, "work":work, "schedules":schedules, "fired":fired, "held":held}))
   487	        }
   488	        // Time remains advanced on failure. Stay paused until an explicit drain;
   489	        // never retry an ambiguous advance to resume pending work.
   490	        Err(error) => {
   491	            let engine = lock(&shared.engine);
   492	            let after = crate::dev_clock_store::saved_boundaries(&engine);
   493	            let fired: Vec<_> = engine.schema().schedules.iter().enumerate().filter(|(i, _)| {
   494	                matches!((before.get(*i).copied().flatten(), after.get(*i).copied().flatten()), (Some(old), Some(new)) if new > old)
   495	            }).map(|(_, schedule)| schedule.name.clone()).collect();
   496	            let mut schedules = crate::dev_clock_store::schedule_report(&engine, &before, &fired, now, shared.hold_schedules || !drain, each_boundary, shared.dev_identity.as_ref().map(|i| i.address.port()));
   497	            for (index, schedule) in schedules.iter_mut().enumerate() {
   498	                if schedule["fired"] != true && !shared.hold_schedules && before.get(index).copied().flatten().is_some_and(|old| old < engine.schema().schedules[index].boundary(now)) {
   499	                    schedule["holdCode"] = "maintenance-failed".into();
   500	                    schedule["reason"] = "clock advanced but maintenance stopped before this due boundary was queued; inspect clock and queued jobs before continuing; do not replay the advance".into();
   501	                }
   502	            }
   503	            let (fired, held) = crate::dev_clock_store::outcomes(&schedules);
   504	            respond(request, 500, json!({"now":now, "advancedMs":milliseconds, "sessionNotice":if preserve_sessions { crate::dev_clock::PRESERVED_SESSION_NOTICE } else { crate::dev_clock::SESSION_NOTICE }, "maintenancePaused":true, "sessionsPreserved":preserve_sessions, "offsetMs":offset, "skewMs":skew, "eachBoundary":each_boundary, "message":message, "schedules":schedules, "fired":fired, "held":held, "reportIncomplete":true, "denied":refusal_json(&error)}))
   505	        },
   506	    }
   507	}
   508

   510	/// system on the dev clock and drive chunks until it is done or parked. This is

    43	      for (const {box,x,y} of clips) {
    44	        if (x) { left = Math.max(left,box.left); right = Math.min(right,box.right); }
    45	        if (y) { top = Math.max(top,box.top); bottom = Math.min(bottom,box.bottom); }
    46	      }
    47	      return right > left && bottom > top;
    48	    };
    49	    const range = document.createRange(); range.selectNodeContents(node);
    50	    const rects = [...range.getClientRects()];
    51	    if (!rects.some(visible)) return "";
    52	    if (rects.every(rect => clips.every(({box,x,y}) => (!x || rect.left >= box.left && rect.right <= box.right) && (!y || rect.top >= box.top && rect.bottom <= box.bottom)))) return text;
    53	    // A clipped prefix must not claim the hidden suffix. Range geometry also
    54	    // works through display:contents and wrappers with visible overflow.
    55	    let result = "", offset = 0;
    56	    for (const character of text) {
    57	      range.setStart(node,offset); offset += character.length; range.setEnd(node,offset);
    58	      if ([...range.getClientRects()].some(visible)) result += character;
    59	    }
    60	    return result;
    61	  }
    62	  function queryAll(root: Document | Element | ShadowRoot, selector: string): Element[] {
    63	    const options = JSON.parse(selector) as { source: string; flags: string; regex: boolean; exact: boolean };
    64	    const regex = options.regex ? new RegExp(options.source, options.flags) : null;
    65	    const needle = options.source.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    66	    const found: Element[] = [];
    67	    const matches = (text: string) => {
    68	      if (!text) return false;
    69	      if (regex) { regex.lastIndex = 0; return regex.test(text); }
    70	      return options.exact ? text === needle : text.toLowerCase().includes(needle.toLowerCase());
    71	    };
    72	    const walk = (node: Node, ancestorSuppressed = false): { text: string; matched: boolean } => {
    73	      if (node.nodeType === Node.TEXT_NODE) return { text: ancestorSuppressed ? "" : textInView(node), matched: false };
    74	      if (node.nodeType !== Node.ELEMENT_NODE && node.nodeType !== Node.DOCUMENT_NODE && node.nodeType !== Node.DOCUMENT_FRAGMENT_NODE) return { text: "", matched: false };
    75	      const element = node.nodeType === Node.ELEMENT_NODE ? node as HTMLElement : null;
    76	      if (element && (/^(INPUT|TEXTAREA|SCRIPT|STYLE|NOSCRIPT|TEMPLATE)$/.test(element.tagName) || element.isContentEditable)) return { text: "", matched: false };
    77	      const style = element ? getComputedStyle(element) : null;
    78	      if (style?.display === "none" || style?.opacity === "0") return { text: "", matched: false };
    79	      const suppressed = style ? style.visibility === "hidden" || style.visibility === "collapse" : ancestorSuppressed;
    80	      const nodes = element?.shadowRoot ? element.shadowRoot.childNodes
    81	        : element?.tagName === "SLOT" ? ((element as HTMLSlotElement).assignedNodes({flatten:true}).length ? (element as HTMLSlotElement).assignedNodes({flatten:true}) : element.childNodes) : node.childNodes;
    82	      let text = "", descendantMatch = false;
    83	      for (const child of nodes) {
    84	        const result = walk(child, suppressed);
    85	        // Block boundaries are whitespace, never concatenated words from
    86	        // unrelated paragraph siblings. Inline RN spans retain their text.
    87	        const block = child.nodeType === Node.ELEMENT_NODE && /^(block|flex|grid|table|list-item)/.test(getComputedStyle(child as Element).display);
    88	        text += block ? ` ${result.text} ` : result.text;
    89	        descendantMatch ||= result.matched;
    90	      }
    91	      const normalized = text.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    92	      const box = element?.getBoundingClientRect();
    93	      const visible = element && !suppressed && box && box.width > 0 && box.height > 0;
    94	      const ownMatch = !!visible && !/^(HTML|BODY)$/.test(element.tagName) && matches(normalized);
    95	      if (ownMatch && !descendantMatch) found.push(element);
    96	      return { text, matched: ownMatch || descendantMatch };
    97	    };
    98	    observedText = walk(root).text.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    99	    return found;
   100	  }
   101	  return { queryAll, query: (root: Document | Element | ShadowRoot, selector: string) => queryAll(root, selector)[0] ?? null,
   102	    text: (root: Element) => { queryAll(root, '{"source":"","flags":"","regex":false,"exact":true}'); return observedText; } };
   103	}
   104	
   105	export function visibleText(locator: Locator, timeout: number): Promise<string> {
   106	  // Build the function in Node, then let Playwright serialize it. Passing an
   107	  // arrow as a string would evaluate to the function rather than invoke it.
   108	  const reader = new Function("element", `return (${renderedTextEngine.toString()})().text(element);`) as (element: Element) => string;
   109	  return locator.evaluate(reader, undefined, { timeout });
   110	}
   111	
   112	// Optional peer: CLI/process helpers still import and run when it is absent.
   113	// Await registration before exposing the synchronous locator constructor.
   114	try { await registerRenderedSelector((await import("playwright-core")).selectors); }
   115	catch (error) { if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code !== "ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND" && (error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code !== "MODULE_NOT_FOUND") throw error; }

+#[test]
+fn untouched_wave22_lists_and_clients_adapt_without_rewriting_owned_source() {
+    for platform in [Platform::Web, Platform::Expo] {
+        for password in [false, true] {
+            let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-w22-adapt-{}-{platform:?}-{password}", std::process::id()));
+            std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("snapback")).unwrap();
+            std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("src")).unwrap();
+            let schema = include_str!("starter/schema.q");
+            let schema = if password { schema.replace("use identity ", "use identity password ") } else { schema.to_owned() };
+            std::fs::write(dir.join("snapback/schema.q"), schema).unwrap();
+            let (app, client) = starter::wave22_screens(platform, None, &Body::Todos, password);
+            let (app_path, client_path) = platform.paths();
+            std::fs::write(dir.join(app_path), app).unwrap();
+            std::fs::write(dir.join(client_path), &client).unwrap();
+            let (changes, _) = leftovers(&dir);
+            assert!(changes.iter().any(|change| change.path == app_path), "{platform:?} password={password}: upgrade teaches ReadState");
+            assert_eq!(changes.iter().any(|change| change.path == client_path), password, "{platform:?} password={password}: only password upgrades remove witness plumbing; guest persona and direct-SDK proofs stay supported");
+            std::fs::write(dir.join(client_path), format!("{client}\n// application-owned edit\n")).unwrap();
+            let (changes, _) = leftovers(&dir);
+            assert!(!changes.iter().any(|change| change.path == app_path || change.path == client_path));
+            std::fs::remove_dir_all(dir).unwrap();
+        }
+    }

+/** Delivery receipts are advisory and never block installation or compilation. */
+export function acknowledgeWarnings(root, version) {
+  const receipt = join(root, '.snapback4/project-warnings-version.json');
+  const temporary = `${receipt}.${randomUUID()}.tmp`;
+  try {
+    mkdirSync(dirname(receipt), {recursive:true});
+    writeFileSync(temporary, JSON.stringify({version}) + '\n', {flag:'wx'});
+    renameSync(temporary, receipt);
+  } finally { try { rmSync(temporary, {force:true}); } catch { /* Advisory only. */ } }
+}
+

    22	## 2. A short common read
    23	
    24	Wave-20 list presentation addition: LLP 3000.000.000.001 §10 defines the
    25	packaged ReadState component; the query hooks below retain their contracts.
    26	
    27	Add `useQueryData(op, args, options?)` in `snapback4/react`, shared by React
    28	and Expo. It delegates directly to `useQuery` and returns `R | undefined`:
    29	loading gives undefined; present, absent and partial give their exact data
    30	(including null, empty lists, false and zero). A denied read throws
    31	`QueryRefusal extends Error`, carrying the original typed `why: Refusal`.
    32	An error boundary may render it; callers choosing inline refusal UI use
    33	`useQueryResult`, which returns `{data: R | undefined, why: Refusal | null,
    34	read: Read<R>}` without throwing. These hooks do not substitute an empty list for loading/refusal. They add
    35	no cache, previous-argument retention or observation ownership. `read` is the same snapshot as `useQuery`.
    36	
    37	A data-only read does not promise completeness or freshness. It is for
    38	screens that show the authorized rows that arrived. Pagination, absence proofs,
    39	and actions depending on fresh or complete results continue to use `useQuery`
    40	or `usePage` and their metadata. The precise API remains unchanged. Skipping
    41	uses the existing typed arguments and `{skip: true}`. Required/mistyped
    42	arguments still fail typechecking; result types come from generated operations.
    43	`useMutation(op).run` is already a typed callable and remains unchanged.
    44	
    45	Add `<SignedIn fallback={...} restoring={...}>...</SignedIn>` inside
    46	AppProvider (bare SnapbackProvider is insufficient), using the same
    47	`useAuth()` snapshot as AppProvider. Signed-in children mount only in that
    48	state; restoring defaults to null, signed-out uses fallback. The existing
    49	AppProvider account boundary resets account-owned state. Children must be JSX
    50	components (React elements), not eagerly invoked component functions.
    51	No bundled login UI or new session runtime is added to the React entry.

   290	## 4. Staging clock
   291	
   292	Wave-20 reporting amendment: LLP 3000.000.000.001 §10 changes the proof
   293	advance return to `{now, fired, held}` and explains persisted boundary holds;
   294	operator authority and clock execution semantics below are unchanged.
   295	
   296	Only a store durably classified staging by deploy --staging may serve with
   297	--clock-offset. Missing/unknown classification is production. This setting
   298	is process-local, with the same signed-duration parser and monotone elapsed
   299	clock as dev. No serve-time mechanism can reclassify an existing production store. Restart drops the offset unless explicitly
   300	provided again; persisted timed-disclosure floors never retreat below their captured value.
   301	
   302	Staging exposes the existing /dev/clock/status and /dev/clock/advance only
   303	with any full service session: these are trusted operator credentials, as for
   304	system jobs. Production refuses even with a service token, and an explicit
   305	production serve offset refuses with E_CLOCK_DEV_ONLY before listener startup. Account, guest, read-only and unauthenticated
   306	requests refuse. Recheck classification and session under the engine writer
   307	before advancing. All fixture, reset, persona and dev-job doors remain closed.
   308	Use the same advance implementation, schedule drain and ambiguous-reply law
   309	as dev. CLI clock advance --release DIR mints its local service authority.
   310	startServe accepts clockOffset and exposes now()/advance() through that
   311	operator authority; it never changes store classification. Each helper control
   312	mints a current staging-time operator session rather than caching a credential
   313	across arbitrary time jumps. Ordinary user sessions retain normal expiry.
   314	
   315	Hot release activation samples `shared.now()` under its engine writer, including
   316	the persisted disclosure floor, and passes that one time through the core's
   317	explicit `adopt_release_at`. A live preview probes the existing filesystem owner lock independently of
   318	HTTP bind/address hints and acquires a stopped-store lock before taking its
   319	SQLite snapshot, retaining that lock through inspection. A rollback preview
   320	reuses its caller's existing ownership rather than probing its own lock as a
   321	live host.
   322	A live preview samples time through the same-owner,
   323	store-bound existing private status protocol; no session is minted or clock
   324	changed. A missing existing-store ownership sidecar refuses rather than assuming a
   325	stopped host without exclusion; ordinary rollback preview can reestablish and
   326	reuse its canonical ownership. A revision mismatch with the private database snapshot refuses as a
   327	racing activation. Store contents and the clock are point-in-time samples, not
   328	a freeze of later live writes. A stopped store's cold activation/preview uses
   329	wall time, with the existing persisted disclosure floor applied by activation.
   330	Positive and negative staging offsets are both covered by hot/preview tests.
   331	
   332	## 5. Evidence

   129	    assert.ok(client.now()>original.expiresAt);assert.equal(JSON.parse(values.get(key)).expiresAt,original.expiresAt+shift,'dirty timing retries even when the next reply repeats the same expiry');
   130	  }
   131	  await client?.close();client=undefined;offline=true;fail=false;
   132	  client=await createLocalClient({url,sessionStore:sessions,device,fetch:fetcher});
   133	  assert.equal(client.session().token,original.token);assert.ok(values.has(key),'offline restoration must retain saved custody');
   134	  if(mode==='close')assert.ok(client.now()<original.expiresAt,'close cannot persist a late unreconciled clock after removing the timing listener');

    29	
    30	// @ref LLP 3000.033#1.4 — nothing passes this gate, including
    31	// exported asset credentials, before the exact bearer/revision/store is verified.
    32	export class ReadScopeGate {
    33	  private accepted: string | undefined;
    34	  private pending: { key: string; work: Promise<Refusal | undefined> } | undefined;
    35	  private failed: { key: string; why: Refusal } | undefined;
    36	  constructor(private url: string, private transport: typeof fetch,
    37	    private current: () => { token: string | undefined; revision: number; store: string | undefined; closed: boolean },
    38	    private accept: (json: Record<string, unknown>) => void, private signal: AbortSignal) {}
    39	  private key() { const c = this.current(); return JSON.stringify([c.token, c.revision, c.store]); }
    40	  get verified() { return !this.current().closed && this.accepted === this.key(); }
    41	  get refusal() { return this.failed?.key === this.key() ? this.failed.why : undefined; }
    42	  verify(): Promise<Refusal | undefined> {
    43	    if (this.verified) return Promise.resolve(undefined);
    44	    const key = this.key();
    45	    if (this.failed?.key === key) return Promise.resolve(this.failed.why);
    46	    if (this.pending?.key === key) return this.pending.work;
    47	    const c = this.current();
    48	    this.accepted = undefined;
    49	    const work = (async () => {
    50	      let why: Refusal | undefined;
    51	      try {
    52	        if (c.closed || !c.token) return readOnlyRefusal("E_READ_ONLY_CREDENTIAL");
    53	        const response = await this.transport(`${this.url}/probe?scope=1`, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${c.token}` }, signal: this.signal });
    54	        const json = await readResponse(response);
    55	        if (key !== this.key() || this.current().closed) return { code: "E_AUTH", family: "auth", message: "the read credential changed during verification" };
    56	        if (json.denied) why = json.denied as Refusal;
    57	        else if (!response.ok || json.ok !== true || !["full", "read"].includes(String(json.scope)) || typeof json.store_id !== "string") why = readOnlyRefusal("E_READ_ONLY_UNSUPPORTED");
    58	        else if (json.scope !== "read") why = readOnlyRefusal("E_READ_ONLY_CREDENTIAL");
    59	        else { this.accept(json); this.accepted = this.key(); }

    33	            (&self.kind, "this session is not live; sign in again"),
    34	            (&self.family, "this session is not live; sign in again")]).is_ok()

    57	/// The durable facts the authority kernel may read in one store transaction.
    58	/// Implementations perform access only; the policy below interprets records.
    59	pub(crate) trait AuthorityTransaction {
    60	    fn principal_generation(&self, principal: &str) -> Result<i64, RuntimeError>;
    61	    fn session_by_digest(&self, digest: &str) -> Result<Option<SessionRecord>, RuntimeError>;
    62	    fn latest_live_family_member(
    63	        &self,
    64	        principal: &str,
    65	        family: &str,
    66	        now: i64,
    67	    ) -> Result<Option<SessionRecord>, RuntimeError>;
    68	    fn work_by_id(&self, id: &str) -> Result<Option<WorkRecord>, RuntimeError>;
    69	    fn work_by_claimed_session_digest(
    70	        &self,
    71	        digest: &str,
    72	    ) -> Result<Option<WorkRecord>, RuntimeError>;
    73	
    74	    fn invalidate_session(&self, digest: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>;
    75	    fn invalidate_family(&self, principal: &str, family: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>;
    76	}
    77	
    78	pub(crate) enum AdmissionStanding {
    79	    SessionNotLive,
    80	    AuthorityNotLive,
    81	    Live(Authority),
    82	}
    83	
    84	/// Resolve the credential captured at admission. Refresh may replace its row,
    85	/// but the family and principal generation remain the durable authority.
    86	// @ref LLP 3000.009#5.1 — action authority is bound at admission and walked inside one transaction.
    87	pub(crate) fn admission_authority(
    88	    tx: &impl AuthorityTransaction,
    89	    token_digest: &str,
    90	    principal: &str,
    91	    now: i64,
    92	) -> Result<AdmissionStanding, RuntimeError> {
    93	    let Some(session) = tx.session_by_digest(token_digest)? else {
    94	        return Ok(AdmissionStanding::SessionNotLive);
    95	    };
    96	    if !session.identity_is_valid() || session.scope != SessionScope::Full || session.principal != principal || session.expires_at <= now {
    97	        return Ok(AdmissionStanding::SessionNotLive);
    98	    }
    99	    let generation = tx.principal_generation(principal)?;
   100	    let Some(standing) = family_authority_expiry(tx, principal, &session.family, generation, now)?
   101	    else {
   102	        return Ok(AdmissionStanding::AuthorityNotLive);
   103	    };
   104	    Ok(AdmissionStanding::Live(Authority {
   105	        family: session.family,
   106	        generation,
   107	        expires_at: session.expires_at.min(standing),
   108	    }))
   109	}
   110	
   111	/// Walk a refresh/delegation family at one transaction snapshot. The admitted
   112	/// expiry is deliberately not an ongoing cap: refresh lifetime and admission
   113	/// lifetime are distinct. Claim leases and deadlines remain hard caps.
   114	pub(crate) fn family_authority_expiry(
   115	    tx: &impl AuthorityTransaction,
   116	    principal: &str,
   117	    family: &str,
   118	    generation: i64,
   119	    now: i64,
   120	) -> Result<Option<i64>, RuntimeError> {
   121	    if refuse_nul(&[(principal, "identity is not live"), (family, "identity is not live")]).is_err() { return Ok(None); }
   122	    let current_generation = tx.principal_generation(principal)?;
   123	    let (mut family, mut generation, mut bound) = (family.to_owned(), generation, i64::MAX);
   124	    let mut seen = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
   125	    loop {
   126	        if refuse_nul(&[(&family, "identity is not live")]).is_err()
   127	            || generation != current_generation || !seen.insert(family.clone()) {
   128	            return Ok(None);
   129	        }
   130	        let Some(session) = tx.latest_live_family_member(principal, &family, now)? else {
   131	            return Ok(None);
   132	        };
   133	        if !session.identity_is_valid() || session.scope != SessionScope::Full { return Ok(None); }
   134	        bound = bound.min(session.expires_at);
   135	        if session.kind != "action" {
   136	            return Ok(Some(bound));
   137	        }
   138	        let Some(parent) = tx.work_by_claimed_session_digest(&session.digest)? else {
   139	            return Ok(None);
   140	        };
   141	        if parent.principal != principal || !claim_window_is_live(&parent, now) {
   142	            return Ok(None);
   143	        }
   144	        let lease = parent.lease_until.expect("a live claim has a lease");
   145	        bound = bound.min(lease).min(parent.deadline.unwrap_or(i64::MAX));
   146	        if let Some(service_generation) = parent.service_generation {
   147	            return Ok((service_generation == current_generation).then_some(bound));
   148	        }
   149	        match (parent.admission.family, parent.admission.generation) {
   150	            (Some(next), Some(next_generation)) => {
   151	                family = next;
   152	                generation = next_generation;
   153	            }
   154	            (None, None) => return Ok(Some(bound)), // Legacy work and development personas.
   155	            _ => return Ok(None),
   156	        }
   157	    }
   158	}
   159	
   160	pub(crate) fn work_authority_expiry(
   161	    tx: &impl AuthorityTransaction,
   162	    work: &WorkRecord,
   163	    now: i64,
   164	) -> Result<Option<i64>, RuntimeError> {
   165	    if refuse_nul(&[(&work.principal, "identity is not live"),
   166	        (work.admission.family.as_deref().unwrap_or(""), "identity is not live")]).is_err() { return Ok(None); }
   167	    if let Some(generation) = work.service_generation {
   168	        return Ok((tx.principal_generation(&work.principal)? == generation).then_some(i64::MAX));
   169	    }
   170	    match (&work.admission.family, work.admission.generation, work.admission.expires_at) {
   171	        (Some(family), Some(generation), Some(_)) => family_authority_expiry(
   172	            tx,
   173	            &work.principal,
   174	            family,
   175	            generation,
   176	            now,
   177	        ),
   178	        _ => Ok(Some(i64::MAX)), // Legacy rows and development personas.
   179	    }
   180	}
   181	
   182	fn claim_window_is_live(work: &WorkRecord, now: i64) -> bool {
   183	    work.state == "running"
   184	        && work.lease_until.is_some_and(|lease| lease > now)
   185	        && work.deadline.is_none_or(|deadline| deadline > now)
   186	}
   187	
   188	pub(crate) fn claim_is_owned(work: &WorkRecord, worker: &str, digest: &str) -> bool {
   189	    work.claimed_by.as_deref() == Some(worker) && work.session_digest.as_deref() == Some(digest)
   190	}
   191	
   192	pub(crate) fn live_claim_authority(
   193	    tx: &impl AuthorityTransaction,
   194	    digest: &str,
   195	    now: i64,
   196	) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
   197	    let Some(work) = tx.work_by_claimed_session_digest(digest)? else {
   198	        return Ok(false);
   199	    };
   200	    Ok(claim_window_is_live(&work, now) && work_authority_expiry(tx, &work, now)?.is_some())
   201	}
   202	
   203	pub(crate) fn held_claim_authority(
   204	    tx: &impl AuthorityTransaction,
   205	    id: &str,
   206	    worker: &str,
   207	    digest: &str,
   208	    now: i64,
   209	) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
   210	    let Some(work) = tx.work_by_id(id)? else {
   211	        return Ok(false);
   212	    };
   213	    if !claim_is_owned(&work, worker, digest) {
   214	        return Ok(false);
   215	    }
   216	    live_claim_authority(tx, digest, now)
   217	}
   218	
   219	pub(crate) fn live_session(
   220	    tx: &impl AuthorityTransaction,
   221	    digest: &str,
   222	    now: i64,
   223	) -> Result<Option<SessionRecord>, RuntimeError> {
   224	    let Some(session) = tx.session_by_digest(digest)? else {
   225	        return Ok(None);
   226	    };
   227	    if !session.identity_is_valid() || session.expires_at <= now {
   228	        return Ok(None);
   229	    }
   230	    // @ref LLP 3000.033#1.3 — revocation generations survive an older executable.
   231	    if session.scope == SessionScope::Read
   232	        && (session.cap_expires_at.is_none_or(|cap| cap <= now)
   233	            || session.principal_generation.is_none()
   234	            || session.principal_generation.unwrap() < tx.principal_generation(&session.principal)?) {
   235	        return Ok(None);
   236	    }
   237	    if session.kind == "action" && !live_claim_authority(tx, digest, now)? {
   238	        return Ok(None);
   239	    }
   240	    Ok(Some(session))
   241	}
   242	
   243	pub(crate) fn live_service_session(
   244	    tx: &impl AuthorityTransaction,
   245	    digest: &str,
   246	    principal: &str,
   247	    now: i64,
   248	) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
   249	    Ok(tx.session_by_digest(digest)?.is_some_and(|session| {
   250	        session.identity_is_valid() && session.scope == SessionScope::Full && session.principal == principal && session.kind == "service" && session.expires_at > now
   251	    }))
   252	}
   253	
   254	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, serde::Serialize)]
   255	#[serde(rename_all = "lowercase")]
   256	pub enum SessionScope { Full, Read }
   257	
   258	// @ref LLP 3000.009.001.000#9.1 — identity fails closed on both stores, including pre-upgrade SQLite rows.
   259	// Lives here, not in `identity`, because authority is compiled without the SQLite feature.
   260	pub(crate) fn refuse_nul(values: &[(&str, &str)]) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   261	    for (value, message) in values {
   262	        if value.contains('\0') { return Err(RuntimeError::Auth((*message).into())); }
   263	    }
   264	    Ok(())
   265	}

   554	fn expire(tx: &impl OutboxTransaction, now: i64) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
   555	    expire_batch(tx, now, u64::MAX, std::time::Duration::MAX)

   558	fn expire_batch(tx: &impl OutboxTransaction, now: i64, limit: u64, budget: std::time::Duration) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
   559	    let started = std::time::Instant::now();
   560	    let runs = tx.expired_effects(now, limit)?;
   561	    let mut changed = 0;
   562	    for run in &runs {
   563	        if changed > 0 && started.elapsed() >= budget { break; }
   564	        expire_run(tx, run, now)?;
   565	        changed += 1;

    53	/** Replace one live full-session bearer token. The server invalidates `token` before it
    54	 * returns the replacement, so callers must adopt or revoke the returned session. */
    55	export async function refreshSession(url: string, token: string, doFetch: typeof fetch = defaultFetch): Promise<SignIn> {
    56	  // @ref LLP 3000.033#1.3 — SDK read credentials live until expiry, without rotation.
    57	  if (isReadToken(token)) return { ok: false, why: { code: "E_READ_ONLY_REFRESH", family: "auth", guide: "read-only",
    58	    message: "read sessions cannot refresh; sign in again or narrow a full session again" } };
    59	  try {
    60	    const response = await doFetch(`${url.replace(/\/$/, "")}/auth/refresh`, { method: "POST", headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
    61	    const json = (await readResponse(response)) as { session?: Session; denied?: Refusal };
    62	    if (response.ok && isSession(json.session, Number(response.headers.get("x-snapback-time") ?? Date.now()))) return { ok: true, session: json.session };
    63	    return { ok: false, why: json.denied ?? invalidResponse(response.status, "session refresh") };
    64	  } catch (error) {
    65	    return { ok: false, why: transportRefusal(error) };
    66	  }
    67	}

    91	/** A session store that forgets when this JavaScript runtime exits. */
    92	export function memorySessionStore(): SessionStore {
    93	  const m = new Map<string, string>();
    94	  return { async get(k) { return m.get(k) ?? null; }, async set(k, v) { m.set(k, v); }, async delete(k) { m.delete(k); } };
    95	}

   106	/** Explicit browser persistence: coherent IndexedDB authority and a synchronous
   107	 * localStorage mirror. Custom mirror adapters need a stable unique namespace.
   108	 * Any script on this origin can read the token; opt in only when acceptable. */
   109	export function localStorageSessionStore(storage?: StorageLike, authorityNamespace?: string): SessionStore {
   110	  const target = () => {
   111	    const found = storage ?? globalThis.localStorage;
   112	    if (!found) throw new Error("localStorage is unavailable in this runtime");
   113	    return found;
   114	  };
   115	  const store = browserAdmission() ? indexedDbSessionStore(target, authorityNamespace ?? (!storage || storage === globalThis.localStorage ? "default" : undefined)) : {
   116	    async get(key) { return target().getItem(key); },
   117	    async set(key, value) { target().setItem(key, value); },
   118	    async delete(key) { target().removeItem(key); },
   119	  } satisfies SessionStore;
   120	  return synchronousSessionStore(store, key => target().getItem(key));
   121	}

   123	export const sessionKey = (url: string, readOnly = false) => storeKey("session", url.replace(/\/$/, "")) + (readOnly ? ".read" : "");

   125	/** The session kept for a server, if it has not expired. */
   126	export async function restoreSession(url: string, store: SessionStore = defaultSessionStore, now: () => number = Date.now, readOnly = false): Promise<Session | null> {
   127	  const key = sessionKey(url, readOnly);
   128	  const raw = await store.get(key);
   129	  if (!raw || logoutEpoch(raw) !== null) return null;
   130	  try {
   131	    const session = JSON.parse(raw) as Session;
   132	    if (isSession(session, now())) return session;
   133	  } catch { /* Invalid JSON follows the same cleanup path as an expired session. */ }
   134	  // A store may condition cleanup on the exact bytes returned by get. Let a
   135	  // changed-snapshot or storage failure propagate instead of deleting twice.
   136	  await store.delete(key);
   137	  return null;
   138	}
   139	
   140	export async function keepSession(url: string, session: Session, store: SessionStore = defaultSessionStore, readOnly = false): Promise<void> {
   141	  await store.set(sessionKey(url, readOnly), JSON.stringify(session));
   142	}

   144	export async function forgetSession(url: string, store: SessionStore = defaultSessionStore, readOnly = false): Promise<void> {
   145	  await store.delete(sessionKey(url, readOnly));
   146	}

   148	/** Best-effort server logout. A network failure or timeout cannot confirm
   149	 * revocation; callers must clear their local credentials independently. */
   150	export async function logout(url: string, token: string, doFetch: typeof fetch = defaultFetch): Promise<void> {
   151	  let timer!: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>;
   152	  const deadline = new Promise<void>(resolve => {
   153	    timer = setTimeout(resolve, 5_000);
   154	    (timer as { unref?: () => void }).unref?.();
   155	  });
   156	  try {
   157	    await Promise.race([doFetch(`${url.replace(/\/$/, "")}/auth/logout`, { method: "POST", headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } }), deadline]);
   158	  } catch {
   159	    // Offline: the token expires on its own.
   160	  } finally { clearTimeout(timer); }
   161	}

   361	  async function release() {
   362	    const previous = owner;
   363	    owner = undefined;
   364	    if (!previous) return;
   365	    previous.pause();
   366	    try { await previous.client.close(); }
   367	    finally {
   368	      if (previous.valid) try { await previous.save?.(); } catch (error) { report(error); }
   369	      const retired = previous.client.session()?.token;
   370	      previous.stop(); await persistence.catch(() => {}); previous.valid = false; scopes.delete(previous);
   371	      if (retired) previous.issued.add(retired);
   372	      for (const token of previous.issued) revokeRetired(token, { principal: previous.session.principal, epoch: previous.epoch });
   373	    }
   374	  }
   375	  function observeEpoch(epoch: string) {
   376	    // A provisional owner does not replace the rollback candidate's duty to
   377	    // observe the shared epoch. Fence every older scope before any callback.
   378	    fenceScopes([...scopes].filter(scope => scope.valid && BigInt(epoch) > BigInt(scope.epoch)), new SessionEpochChanged());
   379	  }
   380	  function fence(scope: Scope, error: SessionEpochChanged) {
   381	    fenceScopes([scope], error);
   382	  }
   383	  function fenceScopes(stale: Scope[], error: SessionEpochChanged) {
   384	    for (const scope of stale) scope.valid = false;
   385	    const affected = owner && stale.includes(owner) ? owner : undefined;
   386	    const expected = revision;
   387	    // Publish before raw retirement emits client callbacks, with every rollback
   388	    // candidate already invalid. Auth and client observers share this boundary.
   389	    if (affected && !closed) hide("restoring");
   390	    for (const scope of stale) {
   391	      scope.pause(); scope.stop(); scope.aborter.abort();
   392	      if (scope.client) void scope.client.signOut().catch(() => {});
   393	    }
   394	    if (!affected || closed) return;
   395	    void enqueue(async () => {
   396	      if (owner !== affected) return;
   397	      await release();
   398	      if (revision === expected) publish("signed-out", null, error.why);
   399	    }).catch(report);
   400	  }
   401	  async function open(session: Session, expected: number, epoch: string, restoring = false, openingSignOuts?: Record<string, number>): Promise<SignIn> {
   402	    if (closed || revision !== expected) { revokeRetired(session.token); return inactive(); }
   403	    const previous = owner, previousVisible = snapshot.state === "signed-in";
   404	    hide("restoring", false);
   405	    // Keep the prior runtime's rotations and pending-write custody alive until

     1	//! The jobs card. Quarry examples are compiled by tests/guide.rs.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.007#21.12 — a digest calls the UI query as its recipient.
     3	// @ref LLP 3000.013#5 — streamed totals, adaptive sweeps and lifetime emission keys.
     4	pub(super) const CARD: &str = r##"# Jobs and schedules: the server's own work, on its own clock
     5	
     6	Jobs do background work on the server: a job body is a sweep, one top-level
     7	`for row in table` loop whose progress commits as it goes. Schedules start
     8	them at boundaries of the server's clock.
     9	
    10	```quarry
    11	table invites:
    12	  inviteeId: principal
    13	  sentAt: time
    14	  reminded: bool
    15	  immutable sentAt
    16	  read <- .inviteeId = viewer
    17	  insert <- deny
    18	  update <- deny
    19	  delete <- deny
    20	
    21	job remind(periodStart: time, periodEnd: time):
    22	  for i in invites where .sentAt >= periodStart and .sentAt < periodEnd by .sentAt:
    23	    if not i.reminded:
    24	      update invites[i.id] { reminded: true }
    25	schedule remindDaily every 1d at 08:00: remind          -- or: every 2m; every 1 month at day 1 09:00 (UTC months, days 1-28)
    26	```
    27	
    28	**A job body is a sweep.** Put every write inside one top-level loop over a
    29	table: `for i in invites:` (every row, in id order) or `for i in invites where
    30	.sentAt >= periodStart by .sentAt:` (bounded by an id or immutable column).
    31	Test mutable columns (`reminded`, `status`, `done`) with `if` inside the loop,
    32	as above; a sweep whose `where` or `by` uses a mutable column is refused,
    33	because a write could move its cursor. A job with no top-level table sweep (bare writes, a
    34	`range` loop, or only a paged `for … first N`) is refused as `job <name> has
    35	an empty body`, with the sweep to write.
    36	
    37	A sweep may read other tables and guard its writes. Here each signup looks up
    38	its game, and a reminder is inserted once: a rerun of the same period writes
    39	nothing (`not exists reminders where .game = g.id and .family = s.family`
    40	works too; the `unique` key is the backstop).
    41	
    42	```quarry
    43	table games:
    44	  startsAt: time
    45	  public
    46	  online only
    47	table signups:
    48	  game: games
    49	  family: principal
    50	  immutable game, family
    51	  public
    52	  online only
    53	table reminders:
    54	  game: games
    55	  family: principal
    56	  unique game, family
    57	  public
    58	  online only
    59	
    60	job remindFamilies(periodStart: time, periodEnd: time):
    61	  for s in signups:
    62	    g = games[s.game]
    63	    if g != null and g.startsAt >= periodEnd and g.startsAt < periodEnd + 1d:
    64	      if not exists reminders[g.id, s.family]:
    65	        insert reminders { game: g.id, family: s.family }
    66	```
    67	
    68	A top-level `for p in recipients:` is an adaptive sweep. The engine chooses
    69	the iterations per transaction, retries a meter-refused chunk at a smaller size,
    70	and parks if one iteration cannot fit. Each successful chunk commits its
    71	cursor and writes together. `doctor` and `run` report the chunk size, retry
    72	count and peak heap. Inner domain loops share the chunk's meter. The sweep index must contain only
    73	`id` or immutable columns. Its evaluated prefix and seek bounds are persisted
    74	with the cursor; its prelude runs again before each chunk.
    75	An explicitly paged `for` before the first sweep stays
    76	in that prelude and does not create commits of its own. Declare per-item
    77	locals inside the loop. A prelude local assigned by the sweep is refused
    78	because it cannot carry a value across independent commits. Rule, constraint
    79	and generation refusals park immediately, without reducing the chunk size.
    80	
    81	A domain `count` or `sum` has no page clause: it authorizes and filters each
    82	row, folds its value, then releases the transient row. All work remains metered;
    83	a job or mutation refuses the whole transaction if a total cannot finish.
    84	Jobs run as the system principal; `as recipient.principal:` applies the recipient's
    85	read rules, audiences, field disclosure and expiry to every read in its block. Each recipient's
    86	live counts and effect key below commit in one transaction.
    87	The system principal is never stored: writing a job's `viewer` (outside `as`)
    88	or the literal `'system'` into a principal column is refused at `check` with
    89	`E_SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL_WRITE`; a value that reaches the column through a local or
    90	an argument parks the job with `E_INPUT: … cannot carry the reserved system
    91	principal`. For an audit actor, make the column optional and write `null` for
    92	system events, or name a service principal such as `'service:reminders'`.
    93	`once` removes the need for a marker table; worker/provider delivery retries
    94	still need an idempotency key.
    95	
    96	Effects are global declarations shared by every file; jobs are module-qualified
    97	operations. The worker dispatches by the declared effect name, while schedules
    98	and `run` address an operation. If `digest.q` declares `effect notify` and
    99	`job daily()`, write `emit notify { ... }`, register `effects: { notify: ... }`
   100	in the worker, and schedule `digest.daily` (`schedule dailyDigest every 1d: digest.daily`).
   101	
   102	A sweep by id visits every row ever written; a sweep by the period visits
   103	the period.
   104	`break` ends the sweep, `return` the job. Schedules accept `every 2m`,
   105	`every 1h` and `every 1d at 08:00`. Periods accept `ms`, `s`, `m`, `h` and `d`:
   106	`every 120s` equals `every 2m`, and `every 90s` fires at exact 90-second
   107	boundaries. A schedule fires at most once a minute.
   108	A job in `snapback/jobs.q` is named `jobs.<name>` in every schedule;
   109	`schema.q` jobs use bare names. `check --fix` qualifies an unambiguous
   110	bare module job. Jobs are not on `api.ts`.
   111	
   112	One `use server` in any module (`schema.q` is usual) enables the whole
   113	project; repeating it is harmless. Actions, routes and schedules naming an
   114	action require it. Effects (including secrets, retry, backoff, deadline and
   115	cancellation), jobs, schedules naming a job and service seats need no directive.
   116	See `guide actions` and `guide routes` for the gated declarations.
   117	
   118	A job is a sequence of sweeps, optionally preceded by statements. Those
   119	statements run at the start of every chunk under its meter; their bindings
   120	are visible in every chunk. A write belongs to a chunk, never the prelude.
   121	A top-level `for x in table where predicate:` walks a stable domain as the
   122	system principal. Every rule passes; validation, constraints, views and
   123	partitions still apply. It runs under the job ceilings (guide bounds). The cursor commits with the rows, so
   124	a chunk is wholly done or not started and a restart resumes at the next
   125	one; the index's columns are `id` or `immutable`, so nothing moves under
   126	the cursor, and rows inserted past the key captured when the sweep began
   127	are not visited, so a sweep over its own table ends. A refused fixed chunk parks
   128	the job with its refusal (`snapback4 doctor` lists runs). No client calls
   129	a job: a schedule fires it at a period boundary of the server's clock,
   130	never twice for the same boundary across restarts, passing `periodStart`
   131	and `periodEnd` when the job declares them (and nothing else). Boundaries
   132	missed while the server was down coalesce into one run for the latest
   133	period; a schedule first seen fires from its next boundary. `snapback4 run <job> '{..}'` runs one here and now.
   134	
   135	A schedule may also target an action: `schedule renewal every 1m as service:billing: remind`. The action must declare only `periodStart: time` and `periodEnd: time`, or no arguments. It runs under that service seat and its action rule, never as the system. Job schedules cannot declare `as`; a job body may use a read-only `as` block (guide actions).
   136	
   137	## A digest is a job that reads
   138	
   139	Seek the period, filter the ordinal. The UI and job share `inbox(c)` and
   140	the separate `unreadTotal(conversationId, readOrdinal, periodStart, periodEnd)`
   141	query. A raw total in each returned inbox shape gets N_SHAPE_METER: one busy
   142	conversation can starve that shape. Inside `as`, a call returns `{ data, next }`;
   143	`for row in all(inbox(null)):` follows every cursor and releases each page. Follow every cursor; one page is a preview.
   144	`all` belongs directly in a `for` domain and needs a list query with one cursor
   145	argument. Start with null for the whole result. It refuses an unavailable read
   146	or any direct/helper/query read that cannot certify exhaustion; no partial total commits.
   147	
   148	Declare totals before `as`, update them inside, and emit afterward. `viewer`
   149	is the recipient throughout the block and its helpers. Persona, user, guest,
   150	application principals and service seats are allowed; `system`, null and
   151	invalid identities refuse. This selects read authority, not a login session.
   152	Writes, `emit`, minting `new` and once asset consumption refuse at compile time:
   153	compute inside, act outside. Reading an asset reference does not consume it.
   154	Nested `as`, a block outside a job sweep, and a return leaving the block refuse.
   155	Declared calls must be Quarry queries over durable data, with ordinary server
   156	availability; native, held queries and all ephemeral reads refuse.
   157	Reads see earlier uncommitted chunk writes; separate client transactions do not.
   158	The clock and meter are shared. Oversized recipients park without partial digests. Maintained rows keep the same restrictions as client query computations (guide maintains): use `value` or member fields. A row assigned to an outer local inside `as` remains maintenance-carrying throughout that local's scope, even after a reset. Declared query results and `all` preserve that row type. A query selecting maintenance state for UI display cannot be called by a job; return ordinary fields or the whole row instead.
   159	
   160	Keep `inbox` locally complete: unavailable unread totals are unknown, not zero. Expired rows are
   161	absent from their expiry instant; tombstoned, own, already-read and out-of-period
   162	messages do not contribute. For system reads intentionally spanning principals,
   163	keep the old pattern outside `as` and prove recipient authority before emitting.
   164	The example's member insert rule lets any member add a recipient; gate it on an
   165	admin role where joining requires invitation (guide language).
   166	
   167	```quarry
   168	use personas alice, bob, carol
   169	effect push
   170
